Title: Histoire du clergé pendant la Révolution françoise; ouvrage dédié à la nation angloise; par l'abbé Barruel, aumônier de Son Altesse Sérénissime la Princesse de Conti
Creator: Augustin Barruel
Publisher: Londres: de l'imprimerie de J. P. Coghlan, Duke Street, Grosvenor Square (the printer's name partly garbled in the scan)
Publication Date: 1793
Rights: Public Domain (published 1793, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item bim_eighteenth-century_histoire-du-clerg-penda_barruel-abb-augustin_1793 (https://archive.org/details/bim_eighteenth-century_histoire-du-clerg-penda_barruel-abb-augustin_1793), the item's own bim_eighteenth-century_histoire-du-clerg-penda_barruel-abb-augustin_1793_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Internet Archive; host metadata date: 1793. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: f02edee4e5cc6434e0d8045a70145f77a7e0debbfd4eccbb39b6c3e6f0d0e9ab.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. British Library microfilm scan (Eighteenth Century Collections Online); good OCR with the long s kept; the title page partly garbled.
Content note: The émigré abbé's history of the persecution of the French clergy, in three parts as his avertissement sets out: the persecution from May 1789 that led to the massacres, the 'catastrophe' of 2 September 1792, and the exile of the deported priests; written in London and dated 10 August 1793, dedicated to the English who received them.
